Table 1:
1) “No derivative loss” (–) means that
N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}\lesssim N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}
holds, and “
\theta
derivative loss” means that one needs
N_{\max}^{\frac{1}{2}+\theta}
to bound
N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}
.
2) This means that
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|
is similar to the indicated quantity.
Here, “
|\xi_{13}|
” can be much smaller than
N_{1}
or
N_{3}
due to cancellation, and similarly for “
|\xi_{23}|
”.
3) “Good control” (✓) means that the nonlinearity (in the
w
-side) can be estimated in
H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
.
Otherwise, it is estimated only in the indicated space.

The classification is based on the following viewpoints:

•

Resonance property.
The amount of regularity gained after NFR is determined by the size of
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\sim|\xi_{13}||\xi_{23}|
.
In this sense, the most dangerous is Case
A
:
N_{1}\sim N_{2}\sim N_{3}\sim N
, in which both
|\xi_{13}|
and
|\xi_{23}|
can be very small compared to the size of each frequency.
On the other hand, for instance Case
E1
:
N_{1}\sim N_{2}\sim N_{3}\gg N
is one of the most favorable, because
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\sim|\xi-\xi_{1}||\xi-\xi_{2}|\sim N_{\max}^{2}
.
Considering the other cases as well, we see that in all the cases but Case
A
we have at least one
N_{\max}
, while in Case
B
there remains an unreliable factor
|\xi_{13}|
or
|\xi_{23}|
.

•

Controllability in
H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
.
By Proposition
3.1
, in most cases the nonlinearity admits an estimate in
H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
in terms of
\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}
.
In fact, the
H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
control will make the argument substantially simpler.
In this sense, Case
C
must be treated carefully.

Remark 3.3
.

The derivative loss in Cases
C
,
D2
,
E3
will not affect the estimates so much, since the resonance property is good enough in these cases (especially in
C2
).
This is the feature of the nonlinearity
w^{2}\partial_{x}\bar{w}
and the main reason why we need the gauge transformation.
In fact, if we consider a similar classification for the nonlinear term
w\bar{w}\partial_{x}w
, then the interaction of type
C2
has unfavorable resonance property (
|\phi|\sim N_{\max}|\xi_{12}|
), which prevents us from dealing with the derivative loss occurring in this case by the normal form argument.

The detailed NFR procedure will be demonstrated in the following two sections.
We will see that infinite NFR argument is necessary only for Case
A
(“high
\times
high
\times
high
\to
high” interaction), while in the other cases an
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
-control is obtained after finite (at most twice) applications of NFR.

In Section
4
, we only transform the cubic nonlinearity of type
B
–
E
; i.e., we rewrite (
4.9
) as

\partial_{t}\omega=\mathcal{R}[\omega]+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B},\texttt{C},\texttt{D},\texttt{E}}[\omega]

(the superscripts
A
,
B
,
\ldots
indicate the restriction of the range of
N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}
to the corresponding set) and apply NFR to the last term.
This stage proceeds as follows:

•

After applying NFR,
\partial_{t}\omega
is replaced by either
\mathcal{R}[\omega]
or
\mathcal{N}[\omega]
.
If
\mathcal{R}[\omega]
replaces, the resulting term can be estimated in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and we can stop there.

•

If we start with
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{E}}[\omega]
, then after applying NFR (and replacing
\partial_{t}\omega
with
\mathcal{N}[\omega]
) the good resonance property allows us to estimate the resulting term by putting
\mathcal{N}[\omega]
in a good norm as
\|S(t)\mathcal{F}^{-1}\mathcal{N}[\omega]\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}}
.
Then, by Proposition
3.1
we can finish the reduction.

•

On the other hand, when we apply NFR to
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B},\texttt{C},\texttt{D}}[\omega]
, it is possible to put
\mathcal{N}[\omega]
in
\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
but not in
H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}
as for
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{E}}[\omega]
.
In view of Proposition
3.1
, we can finish the procedure unless the substituting nonlinearity is
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C}}[\omega]
.

•

Hence, we need to apply the second NFR if the first NFR is applied to type
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B},\texttt{C},\texttt{D}}[\omega]
and then
\partial_{t}\omega
is replaced by
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C}}[\omega]
.
Then, the resonance property for
C
turns out to be good enough to control the resulting terms in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
, and we can finish the procedure.

Consequently, by applying NFR twice we will obtain the equation of the form

\partial_{t}\omega=\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]+\partial_{t}\mathcal{W}_{0}[\omega]+\mathcal{W}_{1}[\omega],

where each of
\mathcal{W}_{0}[\omega]
,
\mathcal{W}_{1}[\omega]
can be controlled in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
We will see that
\mathcal{W}_{0}[\omega]
has additional good properties so that

•

\|\mathcal{W}_{0}[\omega]\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\ll\|\omega\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, and

•

\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]-\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega-\mathcal{W}_{0}[\omega]]
can be controlled in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
(while
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]
is only in
\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}
).

Therefore, we introduce a new unknown function
\varpi:=\omega-\mathcal{W}_{0}[\omega]
and consider the equation for
\varpi
of the form

\partial_{t}\varpi=\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\varpi]+\mathcal{W}_{1}[\omega],

where the term
\mathcal{W}_{1}[\omega]
has been changed but it is still controlled in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.

In Section
5
, we will apply the infinite NFR scheme to the above equation (similarly to the previous results for
s>1/2
) and obtain a priori
L^{\infty}_{T}\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
bound for
\varpi
, which then implies that for
\omega
.

4
Normal form reduction: I. Finite reductions

In this and the next sections, we will see the NFR procedure in detail.
For all of the nonlinear estimates in these two sections, suitable multilinear versions and difference estimates can be shown by the same argument, which will not be mentioned particularly.
Computations in each step will be done only
formally
, and we postpone the justification argument until Section
4.5
.

In this section, we carry out the first stage: the finite NFR iteration procedure to reduce the derivative nonlinearity
\mathcal{N}[\omega]
into
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]
.
As we see in the following lemma, the last term
\mathcal{R}[\omega]
in the equation (
3.4
) is controlled in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.

Lemma 4.1
.

We have

\|\mathcal{R}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}+\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}

for any
w\in H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
, where
\omega=\mathcal{F}S(-t)w
.

Proof.

We begin with the periodic case.
The contribution from the first term in
\mathcal{R}[\omega(t)]
is easily bounded by
\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}
.
For the remainder, it suffices to prove that

\big\||w|^{4}w\big\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\big\|\|w\|_{L^{4}}^{4}w\big\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\big\|\|w\|_{L^{2}}^{2}|w|^{2}w\big\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\big\|\|w\|_{L^{2}}^{4}w\big\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}.

We focus on the first term, but the others are easier to treat.
Apply the Littlewood-Paley decomposition and write
w^{5}=\sum_{N,N_{1},\dots,N_{5}}P_{N}(w_{N_{1}}\cdots w_{N_{5}})
, noticing that the complex conjugation plays no role here.
We may assume
N_{1}\geq N_{2}\geq\cdots\geq N_{5}
.
If
N_{1}\gg N_{2}
we estimate as

\displaystyle\|w^{5}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big[\sum_{N}\Big(N^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{N_{1}\sim N}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\sum_{N_{5}\leq\cdots\leq N_{2}\ll N_{1}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdots\|w_{N_{5}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big)^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big[\sum_{N}\Big(\sum_{N_{1}\sim N}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{N_{2}\ll N_{1}}N_{2}^{0+}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\Big)^{2}\Big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}.

If
N_{1}\sim N_{2}
, we can estimate as

\displaystyle\|w^{5}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{N_{1}}\sum_{N\lesssim N_{1}}\sum_{N_{2}\sim N_{1}}\sum_{N_{5}\leq N_{4}\leq N_{3}\leq N_{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdots\|w_{N_{5}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{N_{2}}N_{2}^{0+}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{\frac{1}{2}}_{2,\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}.

This completes the proof in the periodic case.

For the non-periodic problem, the
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
estimate of the first term in
\mathcal{R}[\omega]
has been done in the proof of Proposition
3.1
; note that we can take
\ell^{2}
summation in
N
without the factor
N^{0-}
.
The second quintic term is estimated exactly as above.
∎

4.1
Estimate of the resonant part and the boundary term after NFR

We separate a certain part with relatively small
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|
as the resonant
7
7

7

More precisely, this part is “near resonant”.
In fact, the “true resonant” portion
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|<1
have already been removed by the condition
|\xi_{13}|\wedge|\xi_{23}|\geq 1
.

part
\mathcal{N}_{R}[\omega]
from the cubic nonlinearity
\mathcal{N}[\omega]
.
Let
M>1
be the threshold value for the resonant part to be chosen later.
For Case
A
, we define it by

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{\texttt{A}}\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{\leq M}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{\texttt{A}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
|\xi_{13}|\wedge|\xi_{23}|\geq 1\end{smallmatrix}}e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}\mathbf{1}_{|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\leq M}\xi_{3}\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}).

(We put
A
,
B
,
\ldots
or
B1
,
C1
,
\ldots
on the summation symbol to indicate the restriction of the range of
N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}
to the corresponding set.)
For Cases
C
,
D
and
E
, we do not use the cutoff
\mathbf{1}_{|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\leq M}
, but instead restrict the range of
N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D},\texttt{E}}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\\
\Phi\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D},\texttt{E}}\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}[\omega]

\displaystyle\;=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\\
\Phi\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D},\texttt{E}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}),

where
\Phi
denotes the dyadic size of
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|
in each case given in Table
1
; for instance,
\Phi=N_{1}N_{3}
in the case
E1
,
\Phi=NN_{3}
for
E3
, and so on.
Note that the condition
|\xi_{13}|\wedge|\xi_{23}|\geq 1
is automatically satisfied in these cases due to the dyadic localization.
The resonant part for
B
is defined similarly, but with an additional dyadic decomposition, as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B}}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},K\\
N_{1}K\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{B1}}\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{1;K}[\omega]+\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},K\\
N_{1}K\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{B2}}\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{2;K}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},K\\
N_{1}K\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{B1}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},K\\
N_{1}K\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{B2}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{23}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}).

We will need the decomposition in
K
to estimate the non-resonant part later.

Lemma 4.2
.

We have

\|\mathcal{N}_{R}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\|\omega\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}

for any
\omega\in\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

For Cases
A
and
B
, there is no derivative loss; i.e., it holds that
N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}\lesssim N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
Then, for each fixed
N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}
in these cases, the localized component of
\|\mathcal{N}_{R}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
is bounded (pointwise in
t
) by

\displaystyle N^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
|\xi_{13}|\wedge|\xi_{23}|\geq 1\end{smallmatrix}}\Big(\frac{M}{|\xi_{13}||\xi_{23}|}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{3}|\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})||\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})||\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})|\Big\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sup_{\xi}\Big(\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
|\xi_{13}|\wedge|\xi_{23}|\geq 1\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{|\xi_{13}|^{1+}|\xi_{23}|^{1+}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\prod_{l=1}^{3}\|\omega_{N_{l}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\prod_{l=1}^{3}\|\omega_{N_{l}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}&\quad&\text{(Case {A})},\\
&M^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{\max}^{0-}\prod_{l=1}^{3}\|\omega_{N_{l}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}&\quad&\text{(Case {B})},\end{aligned}\right.

where we have used the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in
\xi_{j}
’s.
(For Case
B
, we can derive a factor
N_{\max}^{0-}
from
(|\xi_{13}||\xi_{23}|)^{0-}
.)
Since for Case
A
all the dyadic frequencies are comparable, in both cases we can take the
\ell^{1}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}
summation to obtain the bound
M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\|\omega\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}
.

For Cases
C
,
D
and
E
, we have a good resonance condition
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\gtrsim NN_{3}
.
Hence, we can insert
(\frac{M}{NN_{3}})^{(1/2)+}
and apply the Young, Cauchy-Schwarz inequalities, obtaining the bound

\displaystyle\sum_{N,N_{l}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\Big(\frac{M}{NN_{3}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{3}|\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})||\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})||\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})|\Big\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle=M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{N,N_{l}}N^{0-}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}-}\Big\||\omega_{N_{1}}|*|\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\Big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{N,N_{l}}N^{0-}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}-}\|\omega_{N_{\max}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{med}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{\min}}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{N,N_{l}}N^{0-}N_{\max}^{0-}\prod_{l=1}^{3}\|\omega_{N_{l}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\|\omega\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}.\qed

For the non-resonant part
\mathcal{N}_{N\!R}[\omega]:=\mathcal{N}[\omega]-\mathcal{N}_{R}[\omega]
, we apply differentiation by parts as

\mathcal{N}_{N\!R}[\omega]=\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}_{0}[\omega]+\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\partial_{t}\omega].

The precise definition of
\mathcal{N}_{0}
and
\mathcal{N}_{1}
depends on the cases.
For instance, in Case
B1
they are defined as

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{0}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
K;\,N_{1}K>M\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
K;\,N_{1}K>M\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big\{\zeta_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})+\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\zeta_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})+\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\zeta^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\Big\}.

(Recall that we neglect some constants and signs.)
The next lemma gives the control of the boundary term
\mathcal{N}_{0}
.

Lemma 4.3
.

We have

\|\mathcal{N}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|\omega\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3},\qquad\|\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|\omega\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\|\zeta\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

for any
\omega,\zeta\in\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
.

Proof.

These estimates are shown by modifying the proof of the preceding lemma.
For Cases
A
and
B
, it suffices to replace
(\frac{M}{|\xi_{13}||\xi_{23}|})^{\frac{1}{2}+}
by
(|\xi_{13}||\xi_{23}|)^{-\frac{1}{2}-}M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
and make the same argument.
For Cases
C
,
D
,
E
, it suffices to replace
(\frac{M}{NN_{3}})^{\frac{1}{2}+}
by
(NN_{3})^{-\frac{1}{2}-}M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}
.
∎

Remark 4.4
.

The proof of Lemma
4.2
shows the absolute convergence of the sum of localized components:

\displaystyle\sum_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{\texttt{A}}\|\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{\leq M}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\\
\Phi\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D},\texttt{E}}\|\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},K\\
N_{1}K\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{B1}}\|\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{1;K}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3},K\\
N_{1}K\leq M\end{smallmatrix}}^{\texttt{B2}}\|\mathcal{N}_{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}}^{2;K}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}<\infty

for any
\omega\in\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
In the same manner, from the proof of Lemma
4.3
we see absolute convergence of the localized components for
\mathcal{N}_{0}[\omega]
and
\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
, for any
\omega,\zeta\in\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
.

Next, we plug the equation
\partial_{t}\omega=\mathcal{N}[\omega]+\mathcal{R}[\omega]
into
\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\partial_{t}\omega]
.
The term
\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{R}[\omega]]
is estimated in
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
by the second estimate in Lemma
4.3
and Lemma
4.1
, while the term
\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]]
will be considered in the following subsections.
In practice, we do not apply the first NFR to the term
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]
; i.e., as a new equation we consider

\partial_{t}\omega=\mathcal{R}[\omega]+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{A}}[\omega]+\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{A}}_{R}[\omega]+\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{A}}_{0}[\omega]+\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{A}}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{R}[\omega]]+\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{A}}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]],

(4.1)

where the superscript
\neg\texttt{A}
denotes the sum of all the cases but
A
.

4.2
Estimate of the remainder after NFR: Case
E

We proceed to handling the term
\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{A}}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]]
, which requires the
B^{0+}_{\infty,1}
bound on the
w
side.
Here, we first consider easy Case
E
.
In fact, the
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
-estimate of
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{E}}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]]
follows from the next lemma and Proposition
3.1
.

Lemma 4.5
.

We have

\|\mathcal{N}_{1}^{\texttt{E}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}}

for any
w\in H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}
and
z\in H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}
, where
\omega=\mathcal{F}S(-t)w
and
\zeta=\mathcal{F}S(-t)z
.

Proof.

We focus on the term in which
\zeta
replaces the first
\omega
in
\mathcal{N}_{0}[\omega]
:

\displaystyle\sum_{N,N_{j};\,\Phi>M}^{\texttt{E}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\zeta_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle=c\sum_{N,N_{j};\,\Phi>M}^{\texttt{E}}\psi_{N}(\xi)e^{it\xi^{2}}\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\frac{\xi_{3}}{\xi_{13}\xi_{23}}\hat{z}_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\hat{w}_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}).

(The following argument is applicable to the other two terms as well.)

E1
(
N_{1}\sim N_{2}\sim N_{3}\gg N
)
:
We apply Lemma
2.10
with

b(\xi,\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3})=\frac{\xi_{3}}{(\xi-\xi_{1})(\xi-\xi_{2})}.

It is easy to see that this
b
satisfies the assumption for the lemma with
B=\frac{N_{3}}{N_{1}N_{2}}\sim N_{1}^{-1}
.
We then obtain the bound

\sum_{N,N_{j}}^{\texttt{E1}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{1}^{-1}\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

E2
(
N_{1}\sim N_{2}\gg N_{3}
)
:
We use Lemma
2.9
with

b(\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3})=\frac{\xi_{3}}{\xi_{13}\xi_{23}},

which satisfies the assumption with
B=\frac{N_{3}}{N_{1}N_{2}}\ll N_{1}^{-1}
.
The rest of the proof is similar to
E1
.

E3
(
N_{1}\sim N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{2}
)
:
We apply Hölder to obtain the bound

\sum_{N_{1}\sim N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{2}}N\Big\|P_{N}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big[\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\frac{\xi_{3}}{\xi_{13}\xi_{23}}\hat{z}_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\hat{w}_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\Big]\Big\|_{L^{1}}.

Then, Lemma
2.10
with

b(\xi,\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3})=\frac{\xi_{3}}{\xi_{23}(\xi-\xi_{2})},

which satisfies the assumption with
B=\frac{N_{3}}{N_{3}N}=N^{-1}
, implies the bounds

\sum_{N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N_{2}}\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

and

\sum_{N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N_{2}}\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}.

Combining them, we obtain the desired bound
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.

E4
(
N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg N
)
:
Proof is similar to
E3
.
We use Lemma
2.10
with the same
b
, which satisfies the assumption with
B=\frac{N_{3}}{N_{3}N_{2}}\ll N^{-1}
.
Note that the summation in
N
gives a harmless factor
N_{2}^{0+}
.
∎

4.3
Estimate of the remainder after NFR: Cases
C
,
D

Next, we consider Cases
C
and
D
, where the treatment depends on which
\omega
in
\mathcal{N}_{0}[\omega]
is replaced by
\zeta=\mathcal{N}[\omega]
.
We write
\mathcal{N}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mathcal{N}_{1,j}[\omega,\zeta]
, where
\mathcal{N}_{1,j}[\omega,\zeta]
denotes the term in which
\zeta
replaces the
j
-th
\omega
in
\mathcal{N}_{0}[\omega]
.

Lemma 4.6
.

We have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\mathcal{N}_{1,2}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{D}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{D}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}

for any
w\in H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}
and
z\in H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}
, where
\omega=\mathcal{F}S(-t)w
and
\zeta=\mathcal{F}S(-t)z
.

Proof.

We need to estimate

N^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\|P_{N}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big[\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\frac{\xi_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\hat{w}_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\hat{w}_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}}\quad\text{with one of $w$'s replaced by $z$}

(4.2)

for each
N,N_{j}
and sum them up.
Note that
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\gtrsim NN_{3}
in the cases we consider; in particular, we can use either Lemma
2.9
or Lemma
2.10
with
B\lesssim N^{-1}
.
We denote by
N_{\sharp}
the frequency of
z
, the function we want to estimate in
H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
or
H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}
, and the other two frequencies by
N_{a}
and
N_{b}
, assuming
N_{a}\geq N_{b}
.
We also define
N_{\max}:=\max\{N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}\}\sim\max\{N_{\sharp},N_{a}\}
.

(I)

For
\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C2}}
,
\mathcal{N}_{1,2}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D}}
and
\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{D}}
, we have
N_{a}\sim N_{\max}
and
N\gg N_{\sharp}
.
Estimating as

\eqref{norm:NFR0-N1-C}\lesssim N^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim N_{\max}^{0-}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}N_{a}^{0+}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}},

which is summable in
N,N_{1},N_{2},N_{3}
, we obtain the bound
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.

(II)

For
\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C1}}
and
\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C1}}
, we have
N_{\sharp}\sim N_{a}\gg N\gg N_{b}
.
We first apply Bernstein to replace
L^{2}_{x}
by
L^{1}_{x}
with additional
N^{\frac{1}{2}}
, then apply Lemma
2.10
with
B\sim N^{-1}
, to obtain the bound

\eqref{norm:NFR0-N1-C}\lesssim\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim N_{\max}^{0-}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{L^{1}}N_{a}^{0+}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

This yields the bound
\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.
Also, we have

\eqref{norm:NFR0-N1-C}\lesssim\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim N_{\max}^{0-}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}},

which yields the bound
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}
.

(III)

For
\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{D}}
and
\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C2}}
, we have
N\sim N_{\sharp}\gg N_{a}\geq N_{b}
.
Similarly to (I), we estimate as

\eqref{norm:NFR0-N1-C}\lesssim N^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim N_{a}^{0-}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}N_{a}^{0+}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

(and take
\ell^{2}
sum in
N_{\sharp}\sim N
) to obtain the bound
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.∎

Remark 4.7
.

For (II), the latter argument shows

\displaystyle\|\psi_{N}\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\psi_{N}\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sum_{N_{\sharp}\sim N_{a}\gg N_{b}}\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,1}},

which yields the bound with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}
for
fixed

N
.
However, we cannot take
\ell^{2}
sum in
N
.

For (III), the argument in (II) shows

\eqref{norm:NFR0-N1-C}\lesssim\|z_{N_{\sharp}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{N_{a}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{b}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Although we can show a bound with
\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,2}}
by taking
\ell^{2}
sum in
N_{\sharp}\sim N
, this is not sufficient for the bound with
\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}
.

By Lemma
4.6
and Proposition
3.1
, the
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
norms of

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]],\qquad\mathcal{N}_{1,2}^{\texttt{C},\texttt{D}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]],\qquad\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{D}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]],

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],\quad\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],\quad\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{D}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{C1}}[\omega]],\quad\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{C1}}[\omega]]

are bounded by
\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}^{2}
.
We now apply NFR once more to the remaining terms

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{1}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{2}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],

(4.3)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{3}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{N}_{1,1}^{\texttt{D}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega]],

\displaystyle\qquad\mathcal{L}^{4}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{N}_{1,3}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega]].

As an example,
\mathcal{L}^{1}[\omega]
is given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{1}[\omega]=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
NN_{3}>M\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}\end{smallmatrix}}

\displaystyle\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\eta_{3}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})

\displaystyle\quad\times\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}),

where
\vec{\xi}=(\xi,\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3})
and
\vec{\eta}=(\xi_{1},\eta_{1},\eta_{2},\eta_{3})
.
We separate from this term the resonant part defined by the condition “
|\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})|\lesssim|\phi(\vec{\xi})|
”.
Since
|\phi(\vec{\eta})|\sim N_{1}L_{3}
, this separation can be done by imposing
\Phi\gtrsim N_{1}L_{3}
(recall that
\Phi
denotes the dyadic size of
\phi(\vec{\xi})
).
After integrating by parts in
t
, we have

\mathcal{L}^{1}[\omega]=\mathcal{L}^{1}_{R}[\omega]+\partial_{t}\mathcal{L}^{1}_{0}[\omega]+\mathcal{L}^{1}_{1}[\omega,\partial_{t}\omega],

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{1}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
NN_{3}>M,\,NN_{3}\gtrsim N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\eta_{3}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 140.0pt\times\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{1}_{0}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
M<NN_{3}\ll N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}\xi_{3}\eta_{3}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 140.0pt\times\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{1}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
M<NN_{3}\ll N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}\xi_{3}\eta_{3}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})

\displaystyle\hskip 100.0pt\times\Big\{\zeta_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})+\cdots\Big\}.

In a similar manner, we apply NFR to the other terms
\mathcal{L}^{j}[\omega]
,
j=2,3,4
.
For
j=2,4
, we have
|\phi(\vec{\eta})|\sim N_{3}L_{3}
, and thus
\Phi\gtrsim
/
\ll N_{1}L_{3}
in the above definition is replaced by
\Phi\gtrsim
/
\ll N_{3}L_{3}
.
8
8

8

Since the condition
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N
for
\mathcal{L}^{1}
implies
NN_{3}\ll N_{1}L_{3}
, the resonant part for
\mathcal{L}^{1}
actually does not appear if the constants for “
\sim
” and “
\ll
” are chosen appropriately.
We do not need such a precise consideration, however.

To finish the NFR procedure, it suffices to prove the following estimates:

Lemma 4.8
.

For
j=1,2,3,4
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{j}_{R}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}},

(4.4)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{j}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-1+}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{5},\qquad\|\mathcal{L}^{j}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim M^{-1+}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}\|z\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}},

(4.5)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{j}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}},

(4.6)

where
\omega=\mathcal{F}S(-t)w
and
\zeta=\mathcal{F}S(-t)z
.

Proof.

We first consider
\mathcal{L}^{1}
(the argument for
\mathcal{L}^{2}
is almost the same and thus omitted).
Observe that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{1}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
NN_{3}>M,\,NN_{3}\gtrsim N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}e^{it\xi^{2}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\frac{\xi_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\mathcal{F}P_{N_{1}}\Big[w_{L_{1}}w_{L_{2}}\partial_{x}\bar{w}_{L_{3}}\Big](\xi_{1})\hat{w}_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}).

We apply Lemma
2.10
with

b(\xi,\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3})=\frac{\xi_{3}}{\xi_{23}(\xi-\xi_{2})},

which satisfies the assumption with
B=\frac{N_{3}}{NN_{3}}\lesssim\frac{N_{3}}{N_{1}L_{3}}\sim L_{3}^{-1}
.
As a result, we obtain the bound

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}{L_{3}}\Big\|w_{L_{1}}w_{L_{2}}\partial_{x}\bar{w}_{L_{3}}\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{L_{3}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{N_{1},N_{3}\sim L_{3}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{L_{1},L_{2},N_{2}\ll L_{3}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4},

which implies (
4.4
).
For
\mathcal{L}^{1}_{0}
, we can take the absolute value:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{L}^{1}_{0}[\omega]|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}L_{3}}{|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\big|\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big|}\mathbf{1}_{|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\sim NN_{3}\gtrsim M}\mathbf{1}_{|\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})|\sim N_{1}L_{3}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})|\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})||\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})||\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})||\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})||\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})|.

We use Young and Hölder to have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{1}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}}{(NN_{3})^{0+}M^{1-}N_{1}L_{3}}\Big\||\omega_{L_{1}}|*\cdots*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\Big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-1+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}N_{3}^{0-}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}N_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-1+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}N_{3}^{0-}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-1+}\|\omega\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{5}.

This implies (
4.5
).
Finally, by a similar argument we can estimate
\mathcal{L}^{1}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
in terms of
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}
:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{1}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}}{(NN_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}L_{3}}\Big\{\Big\||\zeta_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*\cdots*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}+\cdots\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}N_{\max(2)}^{0-}\Big\{\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}+\cdots\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\gg N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}N_{\max(2)}^{0-}\cdot N_{\max(2)}^{-1-}\Big\{\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\cdots\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\cdots\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4},

where
N_{\max(2)}:=\max\{N,N_{j},L_{j}:j=1,2,3\}\sim\max\{N_{2},N_{3},L_{1},L_{2},L_{3}\}
.
Since
H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}\hookrightarrow H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
, this estimate implies (
4.6
).

We next consider
\mathcal{L}^{3}
and
\mathcal{L}^{4}
.
Recall the frequency conditions for these terms:

\mathcal{L}^{3}:\left\{\begin{aligned} &N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2},N_{3},\quad N_{2}\not\sim N_{3},\\
&L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2},\end{aligned}\right.\qquad\mathcal{L}^{4}:\left\{\begin{aligned} &N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{1},N_{2},\\
&L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}\gg L_{2}.\end{aligned}\right.

The estimate of
\mathcal{L}^{j}_{R}
is done almost in the same way as for
\mathcal{L}^{1}_{R}
.
Concerning
\mathcal{L}^{j}_{0}
, we have the multiplier bound

M^{-1+}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}}{(NN_{3})^{0+}NL_{3}}\lesssim M^{-1+}N_{3}^{0-}L_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}-},

and then the previous argument works, since we have a factor
L_{1}^{0-}\sim N_{\max(2)}^{0-}
.
On the other hand, the estimate of
\mathcal{L}^{j}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
becomes harder, and we need Coifman-Meyer type argument.
After using Hölder to obtain the
L^{\infty}_{\xi}
norm, we can use the multiplier bound

M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}}{(NN_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}+}NL_{3}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}N^{0-}.

Since we have
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N\gtrsim
(others), this bound is enough to obtain
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}
by the previous argument, unless
\omega_{L^{1}}
or
\omega_{L^{3}}
is replaced by
\zeta
.
Therefore, let us concentrate on the following term (one of the terms in
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
):
9
9

9

The other remaining terms in
\mathcal{L}^{3}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
and
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
can be treated similarly.

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{1},N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}\gg L_{2}\\
M<NN_{3}\ll N_{3}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
\xi_{3}=\eta_{123}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})-it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})-\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}\xi_{3}\eta_{3}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\times\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\zeta_{L_{1}}^{*}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}^{*}(\eta_{2})\omega_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3}).

The key idea is the following decomposition of the multiplier:

\frac{1}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})-\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}=\frac{1}{\phi(\vec{\eta})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})-\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}-\frac{1}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\phi(\vec{\eta})}.

For the contribution from the first term, we have a better multiplier bound

\Big|\frac{\xi_{3}\eta_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})-\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}\Big|\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\frac{N_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{3}L_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{3}L_{3}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}N^{-1}N_{\max(2)}^{0-}.

Then, the previous argument for
\mathcal{L}^{1}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
is sufficient to obtain the bound
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}
.
For the contribution from the second term, the bound with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}
can be obtained by the same argument:

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{1},N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}}{(NN_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{3}L_{3}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L^{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{L_{1}\sim L_{3}}\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L^{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{N\gtrsim\text{(others)}}N^{0-}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}.

To obtain the bound with
\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}
, we rewrite this term as

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{1},N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}\gg L_{2}\\
M<NN_{3}\ll N_{3}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}e^{it\xi^{2}}

\displaystyle\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\xi=\xi_{123}}\frac{\xi_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\hat{w}_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\hat{w}_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})

\displaystyle\times\Big[

\displaystyle\psi_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\int_{\xi_{3}=\eta_{123}}\frac{\eta_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})}\hat{\bar{z}}_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\hat{\bar{w}}_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\hat{w}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\Big].

We use Hölder and first apply Lemma
2.9
with

b(\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3})=\frac{\xi_{3}}{\xi_{13}\xi_{23}},\qquad B=\frac{N_{3}}{NN_{3}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\frac{N_{3}}{(NN_{3})^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\sim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}N^{0-},

to obtain the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{1},N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}

\displaystyle N^{1-}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\times\Big\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big[\psi_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\int_{\xi_{3}=\eta_{123}}\frac{\eta_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})}\hat{\bar{z}}_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\hat{\bar{w}}_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\hat{w}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\Big]\Big\|_{L^{1}}.

We then apply Lemma
2.10
with

b(\xi_{3},\eta_{1},\eta_{2},\eta_{3})=\frac{\eta_{3}}{\eta_{23}(\xi_{3}-\eta_{2})},\qquad B=\frac{L_{3}}{N_{3}L_{3}}\sim N^{-1},

which gives the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{3}\sim N\gg N_{1},N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}N^{0-}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{L_{1}\sim L_{3}}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{N\gtrsim\text{(others)}}N^{0-}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}.

Hence, for
j=3,4
we have

\|\mathcal{L}^{j}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}+B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3},

which implies (
4.6
).
∎

4.4
Estimate of the remainder after NFR: Case
B

Let us consider the estimate of
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B}}_{1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}[\omega]]
, which is a little more delicate.
Recall that

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B}}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,j}[\omega,\zeta]+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,j}[\omega,\zeta]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
N_{1}K>M\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\Big\{\zeta_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})+\cdots\Big\}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
N_{1}K>M\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{23}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\Big\{\zeta_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})+\cdots\Big\}.

Lemma 4.9
.

We have

\|\mathcal{N}^{\mathtt{B}}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}}

and

\|\Big(\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,1}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,3}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,2}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,3}\Big)[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}.

Proof.

For the case
B1
, we apply Young/Hölder as follows:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{1}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}}{N_{1}K}\Big\{\Big\|\Big(\mathbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big(|\zeta_{N_{1}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big)\Big)*|\omega_{N_{2}}|\Big\|_{L^{2}}+\cdots\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{1}\end{smallmatrix}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\{\Big\||\zeta_{N_{1}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\cdots\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{1}\end{smallmatrix}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big\{\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}+\cdots\Big\}.

In the same manner, we have

\|\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}}{N_{1}}\Big\{\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}+\cdots\Big\}.

In both cases, we have the bound

\sum_{N,N_{j},K}N_{\max}^{-1}\Big\{\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\cdots\Big\}\lesssim\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}+}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2},

which implies the first estimate.
10
10

10

This argument is not sufficient for the bound with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}
due to the logarithmic divergence in
K
.

To show the second estimate, we use Lemma
2.11
with
p=2
and
q=1
.
For
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,1}
(and similarly for
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,3}
), the bound

\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{1}\end{smallmatrix}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\|z_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}

is available.
Similarly, we have the bound

\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}}{N_{1}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|z_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}

for
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,2}
(and similarly for
\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,3}
).
Then, the second estimate follows.
∎

In view of the above lemma and Proposition
3.1
, the
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
norms of

\Big(\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,1}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,3}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,2}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,3}\Big)[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],\quad\Big(\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,2}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,1}\Big)[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\neg\texttt{C}}[\omega]]

are bounded by
\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}^{2}
.
Therefore, we apply the second NFR to the remaining terms:

\begin{gathered}\begin{aligned} \mathcal{L}^{5}[\omega]&:=\Big(\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,2}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,3}\Big)[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],&\qquad\mathcal{L}^{6}[\omega]&:=\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],\\
\mathcal{L}^{7}[\omega]&:=\Big(\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,1}+\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,3}\Big)[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],&\qquad\mathcal{L}^{8}[\omega]&:=\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,2}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C2}}[\omega]],\end{aligned}\\
\mathcal{L}^{9}[\omega]:=\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B2}}_{1,1}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega]],\qquad\mathcal{L}^{10}[\omega]:=\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{B1}}_{1,2}[\omega,\mathcal{N}^{\texttt{C1}}[\omega]].\end{gathered}

As in the previous case (
\mathcal{L}^{j}
with
j=1,\dots,4
), the second NFR is performed as

\mathcal{L}^{j}[\omega]=\mathcal{L}^{j}_{R}[\omega]+\partial_{t}\mathcal{L}^{j}_{0}[\omega]+\mathcal{L}^{j}_{1}[\omega,\partial_{t}\omega]\qquad(j=5,\dots,10).

Here, the separation of the resonant part
\mathcal{L}^{j}_{R}
is again based on the condition
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|\gtrsim|\phi(\vec{\eta})|
; this is actually done by imposing the relation between dyadic frequencies.
For instance, for
\mathcal{L}^{10}
we define the resonant part by
N_{1}K\gtrsim N_{2}L_{3}
.
The following is a precise definition of
\mathcal{L}^{10}_{R},\mathcal{L}^{10}_{0},\mathcal{L}^{10}_{1}
, and the corresponding terms for
\mathcal{L}^{j}
,
j=5,\dots,9
are defined similarly.

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{10}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\\
N_{1}K>M,\,N_{1}K\gtrsim N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123},\,\xi_{2}=\eta_{123}\\

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\times\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{10}_{0}[\omega]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\

M<N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123},\,\xi_{2}=\eta_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\xi_{3}\eta_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\times\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{10}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]

\displaystyle:=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\\
M<N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123},\,\xi_{2}=\eta_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\xi_{3}\eta_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 80.0pt\times\Big\{\zeta_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})\omega_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})+\cdots\Big\}.

Lemma 4.10
.

For

)–(

Proof of (
4.4
)
\gg

The common strategy is that we take the absolute value and replace
|\phi(\vec{\xi})|^{-1}
by the reciprocal of the dyadic size of
\phi(\vec{\eta})
, but keeping the product structure of
w_{L_{1}}w_{L_{2}}\bar{w}_{L_{3}}
in the
x
variable, and then undo the decomposition in
K
.
For instance,
\mathcal{L}^{6}_{R}
can be rewritten as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{6}_{R}[\omega]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
N_{1}K>M,\,N_{1}K\gtrsim N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{23}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\Omega(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}),

where

\displaystyle\Omega(\xi_{1})=\Omega_{N_{1},L_{1},L_{2},L_{3}}(\xi_{1})

\displaystyle:=\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})e^{it\xi_{1}^{2}}\int_{\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}}\eta_{3}\hat{w}_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\hat{w}_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})

\displaystyle\;=\mathcal{F}\Big[S(-t)P_{N_{1}}\big[w_{L_{1}}w_{L_{2}}\partial_{x}\bar{w}_{L_{3}}\big]\Big](\xi_{1}).

We then estimate
|\mathcal{L}^{6}_{R}[\omega]|
as follows:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{L}^{6}_{R}[\omega]|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
N_{1}K>M,\,N_{1}K\gtrsim N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}}{N_{1}L_{3}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{23}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}|\Omega(\xi_{1})||\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})||\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}}{N_{1}L_{3}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\Big(|\Omega|*|\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\Big)(\xi).

To estimate the
\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
norm, we first use Young to obtain the bound

\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}}{N_{1}L_{3}}\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{1}},

and then insert the Hölder estimate on the physical side

\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}}=\|P_{N_{1}}[w_{L_{1}}w_{L_{2}}\partial_{x}\bar{w}_{L_{3}}]\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim L_{3}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

to get the bound

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}}{N_{1}L_{3}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N_{1}L_{3}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{L_{1},L_{2}}\|w_{L_{1}\vee L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{1}\wedge L_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{N_{2}\sim N_{3}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{N_{1}\sim N\sim L_{3}\gg N_{3}}\frac{N_{3}}{L_{3}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}.

For
\mathcal{L}^{5}_{R}
, a similar argumet (but use Young/Hölder in a different way) yields the bound

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}}{N_{3}L_{3}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathcal{F}[S(-t)P_{N_{2}}(w_{L_{1}}w_{L_{2}}\partial_{x}\bar{w}_{L_{3}})\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}L_{3}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{N_{3}L_{3}N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{L_{1},L_{2}}\|w_{L_{1}\vee L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{1}\wedge L_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{N_{2}\sim N_{3}\sim L_{3}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{N_{1}\sim N}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4},

where at the last inequality we have used the fact that the sum in
N_{1}\sim N
is originally the
\ell^{2}
sum (this kind of observation will be made below without being mentioned).

For
\mathcal{L}^{7}_{R}
to
\mathcal{L}^{10}_{R}
, we first apply Hölder in
\xi
to obtain the
L^{\infty}_{\xi}
norm multiplied by
N
.
Then, a similar argument with Young/Hölder yields the following bounds:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{7}_{R}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N}{N_{3}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{8}_{R}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{2}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{9}_{R}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{L_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{10}_{R}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{L_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

It is easy to see that the summations are performed appropriately in each case, obtaining the bound
\|w\|_{B^{0+}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}
.

For
\mathcal{L}^{5}_{0}
to
\mathcal{L}^{8}_{0}
and the first term of
\mathcal{L}^{10}_{0}
,
N_{\max}
bounds all of

ensures summability.
For
\mathcal{L}^{9}_{0}
and the second term of
\mathcal{L}^{10}_{0}
, the factor
N_{\max}^{0-}\frac{N_{3}}{L_{3}}\lesssim L_{3}^{0-}
is sufficient for summability.
Therefore, in each case we obtain the bound
M^{-1+}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{5}
.

\ll
Proof of (
4.6
)
\gg

Here, we need to exploit the decomposition in
K
.
In most cases (for
j=5,6,7,8
and partially for
j=9,10
), it turns out that we can take the absolute value and obtain the estimate with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}
.
Let us begin with
\mathcal{L}^{6}
.
We bound
\mathcal{L}^{6}_{0}[\omega]
as follows (and obtain the estimate of
\mathcal{L}^{6}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
by replacing one of
\omega
’s with
\zeta
):

\displaystyle|\mathcal{L}^{6}_{0}[\omega]|\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}L_{3}}\psi_{N}

\displaystyle\Big(\psi_{N_{1}}\big(|\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\big)\Big)

\displaystyle*\Big(\mathbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big(|\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big)\Big),

and by Young/Hölder,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{6}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}L_{3}}\||\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big(|\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}K}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}L_{3}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{1}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{N_{1}^{1+}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
N_{1}\sim N_{\max(2)}:=\max\{N,N_{j},L_{j}:j=1,2,3\}
, we can measure any of five
w
’s in
H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
and still have
N_{\max(2)}^{0-}
to ensure summability.

\mathcal{L}^{7}
is handled in a similar way: we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{7}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{3}L_{3}}\Big\|\Big(\textbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big[\big(|\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\big)*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big]\Big)*|\omega_{N_{2}}|\Big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}L_{3}K^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{3}L_{3}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{N_{1}^{1+}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

We next consider
\mathcal{L}^{5}
.
From the non-resonant condition
N_{1}K\ll N_{3}L_{3}
, we have
K\ll\frac{L_{3}^{2}}{N_{1}}
and
N_{1}\ll L_{3}^{2}
.
Then, by a similar argument we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{5}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
N_{1}K\ll N_{3}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{K^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}L_{3}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
N_{1}\ll L_{3}^{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{N_{1}L_{3}^{0+}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{3}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{N_{1}^{1+}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Since
N_{1}\sim N_{\max(2)}
, we have
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}
and still
N_{\max(2)}^{0-}
to perform the summation.
The treatment of
\mathcal{L}^{8}
is similar:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{8}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{3}\sim N_{2}\gg L_{1},L_{2}\\
N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{K^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}L_{3}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{N,N_{j},L_{j}}N_{\max(2)}^{-1-}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Let us next consider
\mathcal{L}^{9}
.
Similarly as above, we have the bound

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{9}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}L_{3}}\Big\|\big(|\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 220.0pt*\Big(\mathbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big(|\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big)\Big)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}K^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{1}L_{3}}\||\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\|_{L^{\infty}}\||\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{N_{1}^{0+}L_{3}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Here, we have
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gtrsim
(others).
Hence, if
\zeta=\mathcal{N}[\omega]
replaces one of
\omega_{L_{2}},\omega_{N_{2}},\omega_{N_{3}}
, then we have
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}
and still
L_{3}^{0-}
to ensure the summability.
Moreover, even if
\omega_{L_{1}}
or
\omega_{L_{3}}
is replaced, the above estimate is sufficient to obtain the bound with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}
.
Therefore, from here we focus on the situation that
\zeta
replaces
\omega_{L_{1}}
, and prove the estimate with
\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}
.
Similarly to the argument for
\mathcal{L}^{4}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
in the preceding subsection, we use the following decomposition of the multiplier:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}=\frac{1}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\phi(\vec{\eta})}-\frac{1}{\phi(\vec{\eta})\big[\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big]}.

(4.8)

The contribution from the second term is easier to estimate.
In fact, we have the multiplier bound
M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}(N_{1}L_{3})^{-\frac{3}{2}-}
, and a crude estimate with Young (after undoing the decomposition in
K
and applying Hölder in
\xi
) implies the bound

\displaystyle M^{\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}}{N_{1}^{\frac{3}{2}+}L_{3}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{N,N_{j},L_{j}}N_{\max(2)}^{-1-}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

On the other hand, thanks to the separation of variables, the contribution from the first term can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\\
M<N_{1}K\ll N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123},\,\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{23}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\phi(\vec{\eta})}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\xi_{3}\eta_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 160.0pt\times\zeta_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\\
M<N_{1}K\ll N_{1}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{23}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\Omega(\xi_{1})\omega_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}),

where

\displaystyle\Omega(\xi_{1})

\displaystyle=\Omega_{N_{1},L_{1},L_{2},L_{3}}(\xi_{1}):=\psi_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\int_{\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})}\eta_{3}\zeta_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{F}\Big[S(-t)P_{N_{1}}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big[\int_{\xi_{1}=\eta_{123}}\frac{\eta_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})}\hat{z}_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\hat{w}_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\Big]\Big].

We first take the absolute value (keeping the structure of
\Omega
) and applying Young, which gives the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\\
K\lesssim N_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\Big\||\Omega|*\Big(\mathbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big(|\omega_{N_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big)\Big)\Big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N\gg N_{2}\sim N_{3}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

Next, application of Plancherel, Bernstein and Lemma
2.10
with

b(\xi_{1},\eta_{1},\eta_{2},\eta_{3})=\frac{\eta_{3}}{\eta_{23}(\xi_{1}-\eta_{2})},\qquad B=\frac{L_{3}}{L_{3}N_{1}}=N_{1}^{-1}

imply that

\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|S(t)\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Omega\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Inserting this into the above, we obtain the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{L_{1}\sim L_{3}}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{N,N_{j},L_{2}\lesssim N_{1}}N_{1}^{0-}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3}.

Finally, we consider the estimate of
\mathcal{L}^{10}_{1}[\omega,\zeta]
.
For this term, we have either
N_{1}\sim N_{3}\sim N_{\max(2)}
or
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\sim N_{\max(2)}
.
When the former occurs, we apply the previous argument for
\mathcal{L}^{5}
.
Noticing that the non-resonant condition
N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}
implies
K\ll\frac{L_{3}^{2}}{N_{1}}
and
N_{1}\ll L_{3}^{2}
, we take the absolute value, apply Hölder in
\xi
and further Young/Hölder to obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{10}_{0}[\omega]\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\\
N_{1}\gtrsim L_{3},\,N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}N_{2}L_{3}}\Big\|\big(|\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 220.0pt*\Big(\mathbf{1}_{|\cdot|\sim K}\big(|\omega_{N_{1}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\big)\Big)\Big\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\\
N_{1}\gtrsim L_{3},\,N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}K^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\||\omega_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\|_{L^{\infty}}\||\omega_{N_{1}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\\
L_{3}\lesssim N_{1}\ll L_{3}^{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}L_{3}^{1-}}{N_{1}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{N,N_{j},L_{j}}N_{\max(2)}^{-1-}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

It remains to consider the case
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\sim N_{\max(2)}\gg N_{1}\gtrsim
(others), and we apply the previous argument for
\mathcal{L}^{9}
.
The same calculation gives the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}N_{1}\sim N_{3}\gg N\sim N_{2}\\
L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{2}\gg L_{2}\\
L_{3}\gg N_{1},\,K\lesssim N\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}K^{\frac{1}{2}-}}{N_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{N_{1}^{0+}L_{3}}\|\omega_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

This gives: (i) when
\zeta
replaces one of
\omega_{L_{2}},\omega_{N_{1}},\omega_{N_{3}}
, the bound with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}
; and (ii) when
\zeta
replaces either
\omega_{L_{1}}
or
\omega_{L_{3}}
, the bound with
\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}}}
.
To obtain the bound with
\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}
when
\omega_{L_{1}}
is replaced by
\zeta
, for instance, we apply the decomposition (
4.8
) again.
To estimate the contribution from the second term, we bound the multiplier
|\phi(\vec{\eta})|^{-1}\big|\phi(\vec{\xi})+\phi(\vec{\eta})\big|^{-1}
by
M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}(N_{2}L_{3})^{-\frac{3}{2}-}
, undo the decomposition in
K
, and apply Young/Hölder as

\displaystyle\Big\|\psi_{N}\Big[|\omega_{N_{1}}|*|\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}|*\Big(\psi_{N_{2}}\big(|\zeta_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\big)\Big)\Big]\Big\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\Big\|\psi_{N_{2}}\big(|\zeta_{L_{1}}|*|\omega_{L_{2}}|*|\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}|\big)\Big\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\cdot N_{2}\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}},

which yields the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{NN_{2}N_{3}L_{3}}{(N_{2}L_{3})^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{1}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{1}{L_{3}^{1+}}\|\zeta_{L_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{2}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{L_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{H^{-\frac{1}{2}-}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{4}.

The contribution from the first term is rewritten as

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2},\\
M<N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123},\,\xi_{2}=\eta_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})+it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})\phi(\vec{\eta})}\psi_{N}(\xi)\psi_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\xi_{3}\eta_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 160.0pt\times\zeta_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3})

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2},\\
M<N_{1}K\ll N_{2}L_{3}\end{smallmatrix}}\psi_{N}(\xi)\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
K\leq|\xi_{13}|<2K\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}}{\phi(\vec{\xi})}\xi_{3}\omega_{N_{1}}(\xi_{1})\Omega(\xi_{2})\omega^{*}_{N_{3}}(\xi_{3}),

where

\displaystyle\Omega(\xi_{2})

\displaystyle=\Omega_{N_{2},L_{1},L_{2},L_{3}}(\xi_{2}):=\psi_{N_{2}}(\xi_{2})\int_{\xi_{2}=\eta_{123}}\frac{e^{it\phi(\vec{\eta})}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})}\eta_{3}\zeta_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\omega_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\omega^{*}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{F}\Big[S(-t)P_{N_{2}}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Big[\int_{\xi_{2}=\eta_{123}}\frac{\eta_{3}}{\phi(\vec{\eta})}\hat{z}_{L_{1}}(\eta_{1})\hat{w}_{L_{2}}(\eta_{2})\hat{\bar{w}}_{L_{3}}(\eta_{3})\Big]\Big].

By Bernstein and Lemma
2.10
, we have

\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim N_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|S(t)\mathcal{F}^{-1}\Omega\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim N_{2}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Using this, we can derive the bound

\displaystyle M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2},\\
K\lesssim N\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N^{\frac{1}{2}}N_{3}K}{(N_{1}K)^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\omega_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{\begin{smallmatrix}L_{1}\sim L_{3}\gg N_{1}\sim N_{3}\\
\gg N\sim N_{2}\gg L_{2}\end{smallmatrix}}\frac{N_{3}}{N_{1}^{0+}}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\sum_{L_{1}\sim L_{3}}\|z_{L_{1}}\|_{L^{1}}\|w_{L_{3}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{N,N_{j},L_{2}\lesssim N_{1}}N_{1}^{0-}\|w_{L_{2}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{1}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|w_{N_{3}}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim M^{-\frac{1}{2}+}\|z\|_{B^{0}_{1,\infty}}\|w\|_{B^{0}_{\infty,1}}\|w\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{3},

as desired.
∎

4.5
Justification of formal computations

Here, we see how to justify each step in NFR argument.

We start with the integral equation

\omega(t)=\omega(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}[\omega(t^{\prime})]\,dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{N}[\omega(t^{\prime})]\,dt^{\prime}

(4.9)

associated to (
3.4
).
By Lemma
4.1
and Proposition
3.1
,
\mathcal{R}[\omega]\in L^{4}_{T}\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
\mathcal{N}[\omega]\in L^{4}_{T}\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
for any
\omega(t)
with
w(t)=S(t)\check{\omega}(t)\in L^{\infty}_{T}H^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap L^{4}_{T}B^{0+}_{\infty,1}
, and hence the integrals in (
4.9
) belongs to
W^{1,4}_{T}\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}-}\subset C_{T}\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
.
We also recall that
w(t)
is a (distributional) solution to (
3.1
), which implies
w\in C_{T}H^{\frac{1}{2}-}(\mathcal{M})
and thus
\omega\in C_{T}\hat{H}^{\frac{1}{2}-}(\hat{\mathcal{M}})
.
Hence, the equation (
4.9
) holds pointwise in
t\in[-T,T]
as
\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
-valued functions.

It is worth noticing that the expression

\mathcal{N}[\omega(t)](\xi)=\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\begin{smallmatrix}\xi=\xi_{123}\\
|\xi_{13}|\wedge|\xi_{23}|\geq 1\end{smallmatrix}}e^{it\phi(\vec{\xi})}i\xi_{3}\omega(t,\xi_{1})\omega(t,\xi_{2})\omega^{*}(t,\xi_{3})

(4.10)

is only
formal
, because the integral in (
4.10
) does not in general converge absolutely for each
t,\xi
.
Indeed, the
L^{4}_{T}L^{\infty}_{x}
control is available for
w=\mathcal{F}^{-1}[e^{-it\xi^{2}}\omega(t,\xi)]
but not for
\mathcal{F}^{-1}[|\omega(t,\xi)|]
.
However, if we regard this term as the sum of localized terms as (
3.5
)–(
3.6
), it is clear that the integral in (
3.6
) converges absolutely for each
(t,\xi)\in[-T,T]\times\hat{\mathcal{M}}
.
We also have absolute convergence of the sum in (
3.5
) in the sense of
\hat{H}^{-\frac{1}{2}-}
for
a.e.

t
, as in the next lemma.

Lemma 4.11
.

Let
